                \n            \n                            <article class=\"article article--details \" >\n                                                                    \n    <div class=\"article__header \"\n            >\n        <div class=\"article__header__text\">\n            <h3 class=\"article__header__text__title title title--h6\"  data-au=\"ag-h3-28\" id=\"section1__title\">\n                Job Highlight\n            </h3>\n\n                    </div>\n\n            </div>\n\n                    \n                    <div class=\"article__content\" id=\"section1__content\" aria-labelledby=\"section1__title\">\n                        <div class=\"article__content__view\">\n                                                                                                                                    \n                                    <div class=\"article__content__view__field \">\n                                        \n                                        <div class=\"article__content__view__field__value\">\n                                                                                            Experts employed on the full-time team may be requested to provide short-term, targeted support to  United Nations envoys, political and peacekeeping missions and country teams, as well as to regional organizations and partners with whom the United Nations works closely in conflict mediation and good offices world-wide. <br />\n<br />\nCandidates should possess a strong analytical capacity, substantive knowledge, and significant direct experience in leading, designing, facilitating or advising on mediation, political or peace processes – whether at the national, subnational or local level. All candidates should have significant process design expertise, substantial and demonstrated understanding of conflict mediation dynamics; as well as demonstrated  ability to work in dynamic and high-pressure situations, and to meet short deadlines. All applicants are expected to demonstrate competence in mainstreaming gender into their work.<br />\n<br />\nSBT assignments are coordinated with the DPPA Mediation Support Unit (MSU), which determines when, where and how the SBT mechanism is utilized. Further information on MSU and the SBT mechanism is available on the Peacemaker website.<br />\n<br />\nProspective candidates should be aware that these are consultancy roles, and not UN staff. Deployments through the SBT mechanism are intended to be supportive in nature. There is no expectation of ongoing involvement in specific conflict settings or political processes, or ownership of products, projects and institutional relationships for members of the SBT mechanism. <br />\n<br />\nFull-time members of the SBT mechanism must be permanently available for deployment on short notice and are not permitted to undertake any outside employment while serving on the SBT. Other types of outside activities require prior approval of UNOPS. The opportunity to serve on a When-Actually-Employed / Retainer contract in support of the Standby Team mechanism is also available to candidates recommended following the interview process. Outside employment is permitted for candidates on the When-Actually-Employed roster. <br />\n<br />\nIf selected, full-time members of the SBT mechanism should know that the composition of the SBT is revised on a yearly basis. As such, any full-time contract may cease after a single SBT term (ca. 12 months) should the expertise provided not be required and / or if the project is not renewed. <br />\n<br />\n---<br />\n<br />\nEstablished in 2008, the SBT mechanism is a service of the Department of Political and Peacebuilding Affairs (DPPA) situated in the Mediation Support Unit (MSU) of the Policy and Mediation Division (PMD). It is a specialized resource that can be rapidly deployed into the field on a temporary basis to provide short-term, targeted technical advice to UN officials and other key stakeholders engaged in conflict prevention or mediation efforts within set Terms of Reference. <br />\n<br />\nLes experts employés au sein de l’équipe à temps plein peuvent être sollicités pour fournir un appui ciblé et à court terme aux envoyés des Nations Unies, aux missions politiques et de maintien de la paix, ainsi qu’aux équipes de pays, de même qu’aux organisations régionales et aux partenaires avec lesquels l’Organisation des Nations Unies collabore étroitement dans les domaines de la médiation des conflits et des bons offices à l’échelle mondiale.<br />\nLes candidats doivent posséder de solides capacités d'analyse, des connaissances approfondies et de l’expérience directe significative dans la conduite,  la conception, la facilitation ou le conseil en matière  de médiation et de processus politiques ou de paix, que ce soit au niveau national, infranational ou local. Les candidats doivent disposer d'une expertise significative en conception de processus, d'une compréhension approfondie et avérée des dynamiques de médiation des conflits, ainsi que d'une capacité démontrée à travailler dans des environnements dynamiques et sous pression, et à respecter des délais serrés. <br />\nTous les candidats sont tenus de démontrer des compétences dans l’intégration de la dimension de genre dans leur travail.<br />\n<br />\nLes missions du SBT sont coordonnées avec l’Unité de soutien à la médiation (MSU) du DPPA, qui détermine quand, où et comment le mécanisme SBT est utilisé. Des informations complémentaires sur la MSU et le mécanisme SBT sont disponibles sur le site Peacemaker.<br />\n<br />\nLes candidats potentiels doivent être conscients qu’il s’agit de fonctions de consultant et non de postes de personnel de l’ONU. Les déploiements dans le cadre du mécanisme SBT ont une vocation de soutien. Il n’existe aucune attente de participation continue à des contextes de conflit spécifiques ou à des processus politiques, ni de responsabilité ou d’appropriation des produits, projets et relations institutionnelles pour les membres du mécanisme SBT.<br />\n<br />\nLes membres à temps plein du mécanisme SBT doivent être disponibles de manière permanente pour des déploiements à court préavis et ne sont pas autorisés à exercer une autre activité professionnelle pendant la durée de leur engagement au sein du SBT. Les autres types d’activités externes nécessitent l’approbation préalable de l’UNOPS. La possibilité de servir sous contrat « When-Actually-Employed » / de mise en réserve à l’appui du mécanisme de l’équipe de réserve est également offerte aux candidats recommandés à l’issue du processus d’entretien. L’exercice d’une autre activité professionnelle est autorisé pour les candidats inscrits sur la liste « When-Actually-Employed ».<br />\n<br />\nEn cas de sélection, les membres à temps plein du mécanisme SBT doivent savoir que la composition de l’équipe est revue chaque année. Par conséquent, tout contrat à temps plein peut prendre fin après un seul cycle du SBT (environ 12 mois) si l’expertise fournie n’est plus requise et/ou si le projet n’est pas renouvelé.\n                                                                                    </div>\n                                    </div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                    </div>\n                </article>\n                    \n                        \n                        \n                        \n                                                                                            \n            \n                        \n            \n                            <details class=\"article article--details regular-fields-label--inline article--collapsible article--open\" open>\n                                                                    \n    <summary class=\"article__header \"\n                    aria-controls=\"section2__content\"\n            aria-label=\"Press space or enter keys to toggle section - About the Region\"\n            aria-expanded=\"true\"\n            >\n        <div class=\"article__header__text\">\n            <h3 class=\"article__header__text__title title title--h6\"  data-au=\"ag-h3-28\" id=\"section2__title\">\n                About the Region\n            </h3>\n\n                    </div>\n\n                    <div class=\"article__header__actions\">\n                <div class=\"toggle toggle--details\">\n                    <span class=\"toggle__icon\" data-tc-icon aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n            </summary>\n\n                    \n                    <div class=\"article__content\" id=\"section2__content\" aria-labelledby=\"section2__title\">\n                        <div class=\"article__content__view\">\n                                                                                                                                    \n                                    <div class=\"article__content__view__field \">\n                                        \n                                        <div class=\"article__content__view__field__value\">\n                                                                                            <style>\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style,\n  #schemaField_font_style * {\n    font-size: inherit !important;\n    font-style: normal !important;\n    color: inherit !important;\n  }\n\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style a {\n    color: #006699 !important;\n  }\n\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style a:hover {\n    color: #006699 !important;\n  }\n</style>\n\n<div id=\"schemaField_font_style\"><p data-pasted=\"true\">UNOPS Development and Special Initiatives Portfolio works with UN partners and Member States. This portfolio offers support to various initiatives and priorities of the UN Secretary-General in pursuit of the 2030 Agenda and the Sustainable Development Goals. Additionally, it helps the UN Secretariat in implementing global programs in areas such as political affairs, climate, counterterrorism and peacebuilding, among others. The portfolio manages the operational support to the Standby Team of Senior Mediation Advisers (SBT) mechanism, along with the provision of project management and implementation services.&nbsp;</p><p><span style=\"font-size: 12px;\"><br></span></p><p><br></p></div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                                    </div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                    </div>\n                </details>\n                    \n                        \n                        \n                        \n                                                                                            \n            \n                        \n            \n                            <details class=\"article article--details regular-fields-label--inline article--collapsible article--open\" open>\n                                                                    \n    <summary class=\"article__header \"\n                    aria-controls=\"section3__content\"\n            aria-label=\"Press space or enter keys to toggle section - Job Specific Context\"\n            aria-expanded=\"true\"\n            >\n        <div class=\"article__header__text\">\n            <h3 class=\"article__header__text__title title title--h6\"  data-au=\"ag-h3-28\" id=\"section3__title\">\n                Job Specific Context\n            </h3>\n\n                    </div>\n\n                    <div class=\"article__header__actions\">\n                <div class=\"toggle toggle--details\">\n                    <span class=\"toggle__icon\" data-tc-icon aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n            </summary>\n\n                    \n                    <div class=\"article__content\" id=\"section3__content\" aria-labelledby=\"section3__title\">\n                        <div class=\"article__content__view\">\n                                                                                                                                    \n                                    <div class=\"article__content__view__field \">\n                                        \n                                        <div class=\"article__content__view__field__value\">\n                                                                                            <style>\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style,\n  #schemaField_font_style * {\n    font-size: inherit !important;\n    font-style: normal !important;\n    color: inherit !important;\n  }\n\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style a {\n    color: #006699 !important;\n  }\n\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style a:hover {\n    color: #006699 !important;\n  }\n</style>\n\n<div id=\"schemaField_font_style\"><p data-pasted=\"true\"><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\">Established in 2008, the SBT mechanism is a service of the Department of Political and Peacebuilding Affairs (DPPA) situated in the Mediation Support Unit (MSU) of the Policy and Mediation Division (PMD). It is a specialized resource that can be rapidly deployed into the field on a temporary basis to provide short-term, targeted technical advice to UN officials and other key stakeholders engaged in conflict prevention or mediation efforts within set Terms of Reference.&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\" data-pasted=\"true\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Functional Competencies</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">UN knowledge: Good knowledge of the United Nations’ institutional structure, mandates, policies and roles, and as well as its relationships with Regional Organizations and other international partners. Demonstrated understanding and commitment to the values enshrined in the United Nations Charter, as well as to humanitarian and human rights standards and other applicable norms, including Security Council Resolution 1325 on Women, Peace and Security and successor resolutions and Security Council Resolution 2250 on Youth, Peace &amp; Security and successor resolutions.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Other: Capacity to travel frequently and on short notice (within 72 hours’ notice) and carry out extended field assignments (usually 2 to 3 weeks, and on exceptional occasions longer). Ability to adjust work schedule to accommodate for significant remote support as well as conducting complementary research and supporting the development of policy and guidance material. Ability to live and work under difficult conditions, but also with the understanding that there is no guarantee of field assignments</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Contract type, level and duration</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Contract type: International ICA<br>Contract level: IICA-4/ICS-12<br>Contract duration: Open-ended subject to organizational requirements, availability of funds and/or satisfactory performance*</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">*Even though the duration indicated in this vacancy announcement is “open-ended” depending on the business needs, consideration may be given to offering a retainer contract. &nbsp;A retainer contract is issued for a period of time during which the services of the individual contractor are required intermittently. The individual contractor agreement on retainer basis will specify a “unit price” per day worked. For budgetary purposes, a monetary cap will be determined when the contract is awarded and issued. Please note that the retainer contract does not guarantee a minimum number of work days, and payment will be made only after completion of work.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">For more details about the ICA contractual modality, please follow this <a href=\"https://drive.google.com/file/d/15TJf0e2aJzEGewuEPWdGbPWsFoPjfUr8/view\" data-pasted=\"true\">link</a>.</span><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><a href=\"https://www.unops.org/english/Opportunities/job%C2%ADopportunities/what%C2%ADwe%C2%ADoffer/Pages/Individual%C2%ADContractor%C2%ADAgreements.aspx\" style=\"font-size: 12pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 255);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: underline; text-decoration-skip-ink: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\"><br><br></span></a></span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Additional Considerations</span></p><ul style=\"margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0;padding-inline-start:48px;\"><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Please note that UNOPS does not accept unsolicited resumes.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Applications received after the closing date will not be considered.&nbsp;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Please note that only shortlisted candidates will be contacted and advance to the next stage of the selection process, which involves various assessments.*</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">UNOPS embraces diversity and is committed to equal employment opportunity. Our workforce consists of many diverse nationalities, cultures, languages, races, gender identities, sexual orientations, and abilities. UNOPS seeks to sustain and strengthen this diversity to ensure equal opportunities as well as an inclusive working environment for its entire workforce.&nbsp;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Qualified women and candidates from groups which are underrepresented in the UNOPS workforce are encouraged to apply. These include in particular candidates from racialized and/or indigenous groups, members of minority gender identities and sexual orientations, and people with disabilities.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">*Applicants notified for interviews will be requested to attend online interviews &nbsp;scheduled to take place from October 5th - 9th, 2026. &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp;</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Terms and Conditions&nbsp;</span></p><ul style=\"margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0;padding-inline-start:48px;\"><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">All UNOPS personnel are responsible for performing their duties in accordance with the UN Charter and UNOPS Policies and Instructions, as well as other relevant accountability frameworks. In addition, all personnel must demonstrate an understanding of the Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs) in a manner consistent with UN core values and the UN Common Agenda.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">It is the policy of UNOPS to conduct background checks on all potential personnel. Recruitment in UNOPS is contingent on the results of such checks.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400;  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Competency Based Questions.</span></p></li></ul><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\">---</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\">Établi en 2008, le mécanisme SBT est un service du Département des affaires politiques et de la consolidation de la paix (DPPA), situé dans l’Unité de soutien à la médiation (MSU) de la Division des politiques et de la médiation (PMD). Il s'agit d'une ressource spécialisée qui peut être rapidement déployée sur le terrain à titre temporaire afin de fournir des conseils techniques ciblés et à court terme aux fonctionnaires de l'ONU et à d'autres acteurs clés engagés dans des efforts de prévention ou de médiation des conflits, conformément à des termes de référence définis .</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\" data-pasted=\"true\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Compétences fonctionnelles</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Connaissance de l’Organisation: Bonne connaissance de la structure institutionnelle, des mandats, des politiques et des rôles de l’Organisation des Nations Unies ainsi que de ses relations avec des organisations régionales et d’autres partenaires internationaux; compréhension et respect avérés des valeurs consacrées dans la Charte des Nations Unies, ainsi que des normes humanitaires et relatives aux droits de l’homme et autres, y compris les dispositions de la résolution 1325 (2000) du Conseil de sécurité sur les femmes, la paix et la sécurité.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Autre: Capacité de voyager fréquemment et à court préavis (dans un délai de 72 heures), et effectuer des missions de terrain prolongées (habituellement 2 à 3 semaines, et exceptionnellement plus longues). Capacité à adapter son horaire de travail pour assurer un soutien important à distance, mener des recherches complémentaires et contribuer à l'élaboration de politiques et de documents d'orientation. Capacité à vivre et à travailler dans des conditions difficiles, tout en sachant qu'il n'existe aucune garantie de missions sur le terrain.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Type de contrat, le niveau du contrat, et la durée</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Contrat: International ICA<br>Niveau du poste: IICA-4/ICS12<br>Durée: À durée indéterminée sous réserve des exigences organisationnelles, de la disponibilité des fonds et/ou de performances satisfaisantes*</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">*Bien que la durée indiquée dans cet avis de vacance soit « indéterminée » suivant les besoins de l’entreprise, il peut être envisagé de proposer un contrat de mandat. Un contrat de mandat est délivré pour une période de temps pendant laquelle les services de l'entrepreneur individuel sont requis de façon intermittente. Le contrat de louage de services individuel précisera un « prix unitaire » par jour travaillé. Pour des raisons budgétaires, un plafond monétaire sera déterminé lors de l'attribution et de l'émission du contrat. Veuillez noter que le contrat de mandat ne garantit &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp;pas un nombre &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp;minimum de jours de travail et que le paiement ne sera effectué qu'après l'achèvement des travaux.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Pour plus de détails sur la modalité contractuelle ICA, veuillez cliquer&nbsp;</span><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\"><a href=\"https://drive.google.com/file/d/15TJf0e2aJzEGewuEPWdGbPWsFoPjfUr8/view\">ici.</a></span><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\"><br></span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Considérations Supplémentaires&nbsp;</span></p><ul style=\"margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0;padding-inline-start:48px;\"><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">L’UNOPS n’accepte pas de candidatures spontanées.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Les candidatures reçues après la date de clôture ne seront pas examinées.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">L’UNOPS ne communiquera qu’avec les personnes sélectionnées pour passer à l’étape suivante du processus de recrutement, qui consiste en différentes évaluations.*</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">L’UNOPS désire favoriser la diversité et s’engage à garantir l’égalité d’accès à l’emploi. Le personnel de l’UNOPS est constitué de personnes de différentes nationalités, cultures, langues, races, identités de genre, orientations sexuelles et capacités. L’UNOPS souhaite conserver et renforcer cette diversité afin d’assurer l’égalité des chances ainsi qu’un environnement de travail inclusif pour l’ensemble de son personnel.&nbsp;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">L’UNOPS encourage les femmes ainsi que les personnes appartenant à des groupes sous-représentés au sein de son personnel à postuler, particulièrement les personnes appartenant à des minorités ethniques, à des groupes autochtones et à des identités de genre et des orientations sexuelles minoritaires, ainsi que les personnes handicapées.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">L’UNOPS désire s’assurer que toutes les personnes peuvent donner le meilleur d’elles-mêmes lors du processus d’évaluation. Les personnes présélectionnées qui nécessitent une aide supplémentaire pour effectuer une évaluation, y compris des accommodements raisonnables, sont invitées à en informer le personnel des ressources humaines après avoir reçu une invitation pour participer à cette évaluation.</span></p></li></ul><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">*Les candidats présélectionnés seront invités à participer aux entrevues. Ces entrevues sont programmés prévus du 5 au 9 octobre 2026.&nbsp;</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Conditions&nbsp;</span></p><ul style=\"margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0;padding-inline-start:48px;\"><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Les titulaires d’un contrat provisionnel (retainer) doivent consacrer environ quatre heures de leur propre temps à suivre quelques formations obligatoires avant de commencer à fournir leurs services à l’UNOPS.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">L’ensemble des membres du personnel de l’UNOPS doivent, dans l’exercice de leurs fonctions, respecter la Charte des Nations Unies, les politiques et instructions de l’UNOPS ainsi que tout autre cadre de responsabilité pertinent. En outre, les membres du personnel doivent démontrer une compréhension des Objectifs de développement durable cohérente avec les valeurs des Nations Unies et la vision de « Notre programme commun ».</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">La politique de l’UNOPS exige de procéder à la vérification des références de toutes les personnes retenues pour un poste. Ces vérifications sont déterminantes pour l’embauche.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Le processus de sélection et / ou l'évaluation peut impliquer une combinaison des éléments suivants:&nbsp;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre; margin-left: 36pt;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Un questionnaire sur la personnalité occupationnelle;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre; margin-left: 36pt;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Test écrit;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre; margin-left: 36pt;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Simulation d'emploi;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre; margin-left: 36pt;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Entretien d'appréciation des compétences.</span></p></li></ul></div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n #006699 !important;\n  }\n</style>\n<div id=\"schemaField_font_style\"><p data-pasted=\"true\"><span style=\"font-family: arial;\">The Senior Mediation Advisor position reports to UNOPS while also substantively reporting to the Mediation Support Unit (MSU) of UNDPPA in support of engagements carried out for the Standby Team of Senior Mediation Advisers (SBT) mechanism. The incumbent of this position is accountable for providing specialized technical and process expertise within one or more fields of specialization, in support of mediation and peace processes. The incumbent will be a personnel of UNOPS under its full responsibility.</span></p><p></p><p>---</p><p data-pasted=\"true\">L’expert(e) relève de l’UNOPS et, de manière plus approfondie, de l’Unité de soutien à la médiation (MSU) du DPPA,, en appui aux missions menées dans le cadre du mécanisme SBT. Le/La titulaire du poste est chargé(e) de fournir une expertise technique et procédurale spécialisée dans un ou plusieurs domaines de spécialisation, en appui aux processus de médiation et de paix. Il/Elle sera un membre du personnel de l’UNOPS placé sous son entière responsabilité.</p><p><br></p></div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Provide technical expertise and advice to clients of the MSU, as well as to conflict parties or other key stakeholders, as appropriate, including but not limited to: the current and projected effects of climate change, environmental or natural resource-related issues on the conflict context and stability, including the impact, risks and/or opportunities of current or projected climate stressors on the environment, communities, infrastructure, economies, and governance; analysis of environmental factors in the conflict context, including as potential entry points; conflict sensitive adaptation and mitigation measures; natural resources management and wealth-sharing, and climate sensitive conflict analysis&#894;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Any other tasks requested by the MSU and UNOPS.</span></p></li></ul><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">*See tasks when not operationally or remotely deployed below</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">7. Adviser on Transitional Justice and Reconciliation</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">When operationally or remotely &nbsp;deployed:</span></p><ul style=\"margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0;padding-inline-start:48px;\"><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Provide technical expertise and advice on issues of transitional justice, accountability for past crimes, and reconciliation to clients of the MSU and UNOPS, as well as to conflict parties or other key stakeholders as appropriate&#894;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Provide process expertise for the design, planning and management of pre-negotiation, negotiation and implementation frameworks, including national dialogue processes and post-agreement follow-up mechanisms. This should include advice on how to promote accountability and victims&rsquo; rights, and/or on context-specific approaches for a society to move towards reconciliation, with a focus on the strategy, design and participation modalities of peace talks in relation to these issues. It should take into account methods and mechanisms for promoting women&rsquo;s meaningful participation at all phases and levels, and modalities for multi-track engagement with youth, civil society, victims and Track II actors;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">As necessary and appropriate, participate in the mediation talks on issues of transitional justice and reconciliation, and contribute to the drafting of the relevant sections of peace agreements, including from a gender perspective and with a youth lens&#894;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">As necessary, conceive, plan and manage capacity-building seminars for the mediation team, parties or other key stakeholders on transitional justice and reconciliation issues&#894;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Prepare periodic reports and relevant thematic briefings for the mediation team&#894;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Prepare update reports, as requested&#894;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Ensure that all activities and written products are gender mainstreamed and youth-sensitive;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Any other tasks requested by the MSU and UNOPS.</span></p></li></ul><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">*See tasks when not operationally or remotely deployed below</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">8. Adviser on Digital Technology, Mediation, and Inclusion</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">When operationally or remotely deployed:</span></p><ul style=\"margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0;padding-inline-start:48px;\"><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Provide technical expertise and advice&nbsp;to clients of the MSU, conflict parties, and other key stakeholders on the strategic use of digital technologies to support mediation processes, including but not limited to: the opportunities, risks, and challenges of digital technologies in mediation and peace processes; cybersecurity considerations and online harassment risks (particularly for women and marginalized groups); digital inclusion strategies to enhance the meaningful participation of different groups and constituencies in mediation processes; advanced techniques for digital process design, online facilitation of negotiations and dialogue meetings, virtual consultations, and multi-track engagement; best practices for leveraging digital tools and platforms (e.g., secure messaging, virtual negotiation tools, crowdsourcing, and data analysis) to enhance transparency, inclusivity, and efficiency in peace processes; and conflict-sensitive approaches to digital engagement, including mitigating the impact of misinformation, hate speech, and algorithmic biases.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Provide process expertise&nbsp;for the design, planning and management of digital issues within pre-negotiation, negotiation and implementation frameworks, including national dialogue processes and post-agreement follow-up mechanisms and monitoring. This expertise should include methods for promoting women's meaningful digital participation at all phases and addressing the impacts of the malicious use of digital technologies, including on civilians and civilian infrastructure.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Support mediation teams&nbsp;in mediation, facilitation and dialogue processes by providing real-time technical support for digital components of negotiations, contributing to the drafting of relevant sections of peace agreements that address digital considerations such as limiting the malicious use of digital technologies, or advising on digital implementation and monitoring mechanisms.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Conduct capacity-building&nbsp;for mediation teams, parties, and stakeholders on: digital process design, facilitation, and risk mitigation; malicious uses of digital technologies by conflict parties and possible options within mediation processes to restrict their use; and digital tools and platforms to promote gender sensitive and youth inclusive processes.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Prepare periodic reports and relevant thematic briefings&nbsp;for the mediation team on the effectiveness of digital interventions and emerging technological trends relevant to peace processes as needed.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Ensure that all activities and written products are gender-responsive, conflict-sensitive, and accessible&nbsp;to stakeholders with varying levels of digital literacy.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Research and document emerging technologies, tools, and platforms that may enhance mediation processes, including evaluation of their applicability in various conflict contexts.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Contribute to operational guidance, training materials, and policy frameworks on digital technologies and mediation, addressing both technical considerations and process design implications.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Contribute to MSU's network of experts&nbsp;in digital technologies and mediation, fostering partnerships with relevant technology providers, academic institutions, and civil society organizations.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Contribute to training sessions&nbsp;for UN staff and partners on digital facilitation, use of digital technologies by conflict parties, and digital monitoring and implementation mechanisms.Any other tasks&nbsp;requested by the MSU and UNOPS.</span></p></li></ul><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">*See tasks when not operationally or remotely deployed below</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">*Applicable to all Advisor positions when not operationally or remotely &nbsp;deployed:</span></p><ul style=\"margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0;padding-inline-start:48px;\"><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Research and coordinate lessons learned reviews of best practices in the area of expertise&#894;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Research thematic and comparative briefings to support ongoing or planned mediation efforts&#894;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Donner des avis d'expert et des conseils techniques sur la conduite et la conception des processus de m&eacute;diation inclusifs, de facilitation et de dialogue aux clients du MSU et l'UNOPS et, au besoin, aux parties au conflit et aux autres intervenants cl&eacute;s ;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 10pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Apporter une expertise pratique en mati&egrave;re de processus, de conception, d&rsquo;organisation et de gestion des plans de pr&eacute;-n&eacute;gociation, de n&eacute;gociation et de mise en &oelig;uvre des accords, &agrave; tous les niveaux, dans le cadre de dialogues nationaux, d&rsquo;efforts de m&eacute;diation aux niveaux local et sous-national, et avec les m&eacute;canismes de concertation et de suivi, ou les modalit&eacute;s d'engagement des diff&eacute;rents acteurs, y compris les parties au conflit, la soci&eacute;t&eacute; civile ou les acteurs &eacute;conomiques. Ces avis devront notamment tenir compte des m&eacute;thodes et m&eacute;canismes de promotion de la participation significative des femmes &agrave; toutes les phases et &agrave; tous les niveaux et des modalit&eacute;s d'un engagement multipiste avec les jeunes, la soci&eacute;t&eacute; civile, les acteurs &eacute;conomiques et les acteurs non officiels;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 10pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Participer, au besoin, aux activit&eacute;s de m&eacute;diation, de facilitation et de dialogue, et contribuer &agrave; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Selon le besoin, concevoir, organiser et g&eacute;rer des s&eacute;minaires de renforcement des capacit&eacute;s sur la m&eacute;diation, la facilitation et le dialogue inclusifs et l'analyse des conflits et/ou l'analyse politique &agrave; plusieurs niveaux, &agrave; l&rsquo;intention notamment de l&rsquo;&eacute;quipe de m&eacute;diation, des parties en pr&eacute;sence et d&rsquo;autres intervenants cl&eacute;s;&nbsp;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; l&rsquo;intention de l&rsquo;&eacute;quipe participant &agrave; la m&eacute;diation;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 10pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Apporter une expertise pratique en mati&egrave;re de processus, de conception, d&rsquo;organisation et de gestion des cadres inclusifs de pr&eacute;-n&eacute;gociation, de n&eacute;gociation et de mise en &oelig;uvre des accords, notamment les processus de dialogue national et les m&eacute;canismes de suivi, afin de faciliter l&rsquo;int&eacute;gration des femmes. Ces avis devront notamment tenir compte des m&eacute;thodes et m&eacute;canismes de promotion de la participation significative des femmes &agrave; toutes les phases et &agrave; tous les niveaux et les modalit&eacute;s d'un engagement multipiste avec les jeunes, la soci&eacute;t&eacute; la paix et &agrave; la s&eacute;curit&eacute;;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; Sp&eacute;cialiste du changement climatique, de l`environnement et des ressources naturelles</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Fournir une expertise technique et des conseils aux clients du MSU, ainsi qu'aux parties au conflit ou &agrave; d'autres parties prenantes cl&eacute;s, le cas &eacute;ch&eacute;ant, y compris, mais sans s'y limiter : les effets actuels et projet&eacute;s du changement climatique sur le contexte du conflit et la stabilit&eacute;, y compris l'impact, les risques et/ou opportunit&eacute;s des facteurs de stress climatiques actuels ou projet&eacute;s sur l'environnement, les communaut&eacute;s, les infrastructures, les &eacute;conomies et la gouvernance ; analyse des facteurs environnementaux dans le contexte du conflit, notamment en tant que points d'entr&eacute;e potentiels ; mesures d&rsquo;adaptation et d&rsquo;att&eacute;nuation sensibles aux conflits ; gestion des ressources naturelles et partage des richesses, et analyse des conflits sensibles au climat;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Fournir une expertise en mati&egrave;re de processus pour la conception, la planification et la gestion des cadres de pr&eacute;-n&eacute;gociation, de n&eacute;gociation et de mise en &oelig;uvre, y compris les processus de dialogue national et les m&eacute;canismes de suivi post-accord pour faire face aux effets n&eacute;fastes du changement climatique ou des facteurs environnementaux sur les conflits ou les diff&eacute;rends, y compris sur les ressources naturelles. Cette expertise devrait inclure des m&eacute;thodes et des m&eacute;canismes visant &agrave; promouvoir une participation significative des femmes &agrave; toutes les phases et &agrave; tous les niveaux, ainsi que des modalit&eacute;s d'engagement multivoie avec les jeunes, la soci&eacute;t&eacute; civile, le secteur priv&eacute; et d'autres acteurs de la voie II;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; la r&eacute;daction des sections pertinentes des accords de paix, en tenant compte des dimensions de genre pertinentes, ainsi que des d&eacute;fis et opportunit&eacute;s potentiels li&eacute;s &agrave; l&rsquo;intention notamment de l&rsquo;&eacute;quipe de m&eacute;diation, des parties en conflit, et d&rsquo;autres acteurs concern&eacute;s;&nbsp;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc;
