color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">8. Conseiller (&egrave;re) en technologie num&eacute;rique, m&eacute;diation et inclusion</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">En cas de d&eacute;placement op&eacute;rationnel ou &agrave; distance:</span></p><ul style=\"margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0;padding-inline-start:48px;\"><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 11pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Fournir une expertise technique et des conseils aux clients du MSU, aux parties au conflit et &agrave; d'autres parties prenantes cl&eacute;s sur l'utilisation strat&eacute;gique des technologies num&eacute;riques pour soutenir les processus de m&eacute;diation, y compris, mais sans s'y limiter : les opportunit&eacute;s, les risques et les d&eacute;fis des technologies num&eacute;riques dans les processus de m&eacute;diation et de paix ; les consid&eacute;rations de cybers&eacute;curit&eacute; et les risques de harc&egrave;lement en ligne (en particulier pour les femmes et les groupes marginalis&eacute;s); les strat&eacute;gies d'inclusion num&eacute;rique pour am&eacute;liorer la participation significative de diff&eacute;rents groupes et circonscriptions dans les processus de m&eacute;diation; les techniques avanc&eacute;es pour la conception de processus num&eacute;riques, la facilitation en ligne des n&eacute;gociations et des r&eacute;unions de dialogue, les consultations virtuelles et l'engagement multi-pistes; les meilleures pratiques pour tirer parti des outils et des plateformes num&eacute;riques (par ex. , la messagerie s&eacute;curis&eacute;e, les outils de n&eacute;gociation virtuelle, le crowdsourcing et l'analyse de donn&eacute;es) pour am&eacute;liorer la transparence, l'inclusivit&eacute; et l'efficacit&eacute; des processus de paix; et les approches de l'engagement num&eacute;rique sensibles aux conflits, y compris l'att&eacute;nuation de l'impact de la d&eacute;sinformation, du discours haineux et des biais algorithmiques;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Fournir une expertise en mati&egrave;re de processus pour la conception, la planification et la gestion des questions num&eacute;riques dans les cadres de pr&eacute;-n&eacute;gociation, de n&eacute;gociation et de mise en &oelig;uvre, y compris les processus de dialogue national et les m&eacute;canismes de suivi et de contr&ocirc;le apr&egrave;s l'accord. Cette expertise devrait inclure des m&eacute;thodes visant &agrave; promouvoir la participation num&eacute;rique significative des femmes &agrave; toutes les phases et &agrave; traiter les impacts de l'usage malveillant des technologies , y compris sur les civils et les infrastructures civiles ; Appuyer les &eacute;quipes de m&eacute;diation dans les processus de m&eacute;diation, de facilitation et de dialogue en fournissant un appui technique en temps r&eacute;el pour les composantes num&eacute;riques des n&eacute;gociations, en contribuant &agrave; la r&eacute;daction des sections pertinentes des accords de paix qui traitent des consid&eacute;rations num&eacute;riques telles que la limitation de l'usage malveillant des technologies num&eacute;riques, ou en donnant des conseils sur la mise en &oelig;uvre num&eacute;rique et les m&eacute;canismes de suivi; Renforcer les capacit&eacute;s des &eacute;quipes de m&eacute;diation, des parties et des parties prenantes en ce qui concerne : la conception, la facilitation et l'att&eacute;nuation des risques des processus num&eacute;riques ; les usages malveillants des technologies num&eacute;riques par les parties au conflit et les options possibles dans le cadre des processus de m&eacute;diation pour restreindre leur utilisation ; et les outils et plateformes num&eacute;riques pour promouvoir des processus tenant compte de l'&eacute;galit&eacute; des sexes et de l'inclusion des jeunes;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 11pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Pr&eacute;parer des rapports p&eacute;riodiques et des expos&eacute;s th&eacute;matiques pertinents pour l'&eacute;quipe de m&eacute;diation sur l'efficacit&eacute; des interventions num&eacute;riques et les tendances technologiques &eacute;mergentes pertinentes pour les processus de paix, selon les besoins;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 11pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Veiller &agrave; ce que toutes les activit&eacute;s et tous les produits &eacute;crits tiennent compte de la dimension de genre, des conflits et soient accessibles aux parties prenantes ayant diff&eacute;rents niveaux de connaissances num&eacute;riques;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Rechercher et documenter les technologies, outils et plateformes &eacute;mergents susceptibles d'am&eacute;liorer les processus de m&eacute;diation, y compris l'&eacute;valuation de leur applicabilit&eacute; dans divers contextes de conflit;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Contribuer &agrave; l'&eacute;laboration d'orientations op&eacute;rationnelles, de mat&eacute;riel de formation et de cadres politiques sur les technologies num&eacute;riques et la m&eacute;diation, en tenant compte &agrave; la fois des consid&eacute;rations techniques et des implications en mati&egrave;re de conception des processus;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Contribuer au r&eacute;seau d'experts du MSU dans le domaine des technologies num&eacute;riques et de la m&eacute;diation, en favorisant les partenariats avec les fournisseurs de technologies, les &eacute;tablissements universitaires et les organisations de la soci&eacute;t&eacute; civile concern&eacute;s;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Contribuer &agrave; des sessions de formation pour le personnel des Nations unies et les partenaires sur la facilitation num&eacute;rique, l'utilisation des technologies num&eacute;riques par les parties au conflit, et les m&eacute;canismes num&eacute;riques de suivi et de mise en &oelig;uvre.</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 11pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Accomplir toute les autres t&acirc;che demand&eacute;es par l&rsquo;Unit&eacute; d&rsquo;appui &agrave; la m&eacute;diation et l&rsquo;UNOPS.</span></p></li></ul><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.54;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">*Voir ci-dessous les t&acirc;ches qui ne sont pas op&eacute;rationnelles ou d&eacute;ploy&eacute;es &agrave; distance</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">*Applicable &agrave; tous les postes de conseiller lorsqu'ils ne sont pas d&eacute;ploy&eacute;s op&eacute;rationnellement ou &agrave; distance :</span></p><ul style=\"margin-top:0;margin-bottom:0;padding-inline-start:48px;\"><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 10pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Fournir aux &eacute;quipes de m&eacute;diation en mission, aux parties en pr&eacute;sence et aux autres intervenants cl&eacute;s, selon le cas, par t&eacute;l&eacute;phone, en ligne ou par &eacute;crit, des avis et des conseils techniques dans le domaine d&rsquo;expertise;&nbsp;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Effectuer des &eacute;tudes pr&eacute;alables sur les meilleures pratiques tir&eacute;es de l&rsquo;exp&eacute;rience dans le domaine d&rsquo;expertise et coordonner ces examens;&nbsp;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 10pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Se documenter pour faire des expos&eacute;s th&eacute;matiques et comparatifs &agrave; l&rsquo;appui d&rsquo;activit&eacute;s de m&eacute;diation en cours ou pr&eacute;vues;&nbsp;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 10pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Produire et/ou contribuer &agrave; la r&eacute;daction de notes d&rsquo;orientation op&eacute;rationnelle et des supports de formation connexes dans le domaine d&rsquo;expertise;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 10pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre;\"><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Pr&eacute;parer et organiser des stages de formation dans le domaine d&rsquo;expertise pour le personnel de mission;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 10pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; genre et soient sensibles aux jeunes;</span></p></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"list-style-type: disc; font-size: 10pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 400; font-style: normal;                                                                                      \n                                    <div class=\"article__content__view__field \">\n                                        \n                                        <div class=\"article__content__view__field__value\">\n                                                                                            <style>\n  .competency-scope .competency-group {\n    margin-bottom: 0 !important;\n  }\n  .competency-scope .competency {\n    max-width: 900px;\n    display: flex;\n    align-items: flex-start;\n    gap: 15px;\n    font-family: \"Open Sans\", sans-serif;\n    line-height: 1.5;\n    margin-bottom: 24px;\n  }\n  .competency-scope .competency__icon {\n    width: 90px;\n    height: 90px;\n    flex: 0 0 90px;\n  }\n  .competency-scope .competency__text {\n    flex: 1;\n  text-align: justify !important;          \n  text-justify: inter-word;                 \n  hyphens: auto;                            \n  overflow-wrap: anywhere; \n  }\n</style>\n<div id=\"schemaFieldContainer\" class=\"competency-scope\">\n  <div class=\"competency-group strategic\">\n    <div class=\"competency\">\n      <img class=\"competency__icon\" src=\"https://storage.googleapis.com/avature_icons/Competencies/StrategicPerspective.png\" alt=\"Strategic Perspective\">\n      <div class=\"competency__text\">\n        Develops and implements sustainable business strategies, thinks long term and externally in order to positively shape the organisation. Anticipates and perceives the impact and implications of future decisions and activities on other parts of the organisation.\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                                    </div>\n                                                                                                                                                                    \n                                    <div class=\"article__content__view__field \">\n                                        \n                                        <div class=\"article__content__view__field__value\">\n                                                                                            <style>\n  .competency-scope .competency-group {\n    margin-bottom: 40px;\n  }\n\n  .competency-scope .competency {\n    max-width: 900px;\n    display: flex;\n    align-items: flex-start;\n    gap: 15px;\n    font-family: \"Open Sans\", sans-serif;\n    line-height: 1.5;\n    text-align: justify;\n    margin-bottom: 24px;\n  }\n\n  .competency-scope .competency__icon {\n    width: 90px;\n    height: 90px;\n    flex: 0 0 90px;\n  }\n\n  .competency-scope .competency__text {\n    flex: 1;\n  }\n</style>\n\n\n<div id=\"anotherSection\" class=\"competency-scope\">\n  <div class=\"competency-group others\">\n\n    <div class=\"competency\">\n      <img class=\"competency__icon\" src=\"https://storage.googleapis.com/avature_icons/Competencies/IntegrityAndInclusion.png\" alt=\"Respect\">\n      <div class=\"competency__text\">\n        Treats all individuals with respect; responds sensitively to differences and encourages others to do the same. Upholds organisational and ethical norms. Maintains high standards of trustworthiness. Role model for diversity and inclusion.\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"competency\">\n      <img class=\"competency__icon\" src=\"https://storage.googleapis.com/avature_icons/Competencies/LeadingSelfAndOthers.png\" alt=\"Collaboration\">\n      <div class=\"competency__text\">\n        Acts as a positive role model contributing to the team spirit. Collaborates and supports the development of others. <strong>For people managers only:</strong> Acts as positive leadership role model, motivates, directs and inspires others to succeed, utilizing appropriate leadership styles.\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"competency\">\n      <img class=\"competency__icon\" src=\"https://storage.googleapis.com/avature_icons/Competencies/Partnering.png\" alt=\"Partnerships\">\n      <div class=\"competency__text\">\n        Demonstrates understanding of the impact of own role on all partners and always puts the end beneficiary first. Builds and maintains strong external relationships and is a competent partner for others (if relevant to the role).\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"competency\">\n      <img class=\"competency__icon\" src=\"https://storage.googleapis.com/avature_icons/Competencies/ResultsOrientation.png\" alt=\"Excellence\">\n      <div class=\"competency__text\">\n        Efficiently establishes an appropriate course of action for self and/or others to accomplish a goal. Actions lead to total task accomplishment through concern for quality in all areas. Sees opportunities and takes the initiative to act on them. Understands that responsible use of resources maximizes our impact on our beneficiaries.\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"competency\">\n      <img class=\"competency__icon\" src=\"https://storage.googleapis.com/avature_icons/Competencies/Agility.png\" alt=\"Adaptability\">\n      <div class=\"competency__text\">\n        Open to change and flexible in a fast paced environment. Effectively adapts own approach to suit changing circumstances or requirements. Reflects on experiences and modifies own behavior. Performance is consistent, even under pressure. Always pursues continuous improvements.\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"competency\">\n      <img class=\"competency__icon\" src=\"https://storage.googleapis.com/avature_icons/Competencies/SolutionFocused.png\" alt=\"Decision-making\">\n      <div class=\"competency__text\">\n        Evaluates data and courses of action to reach logical, pragmatic decisions. Takes an unbiased, rational approach with calculated risks. Applies innovation and creativity to problem-solving.\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"competency\">\n      <img class=\"competency__icon\" src=\"https://storage.googleapis.com/avature_icons/Competencies/EffectiveCommunication.png\" alt=\"Communication\">\n      <div class=\"competency__text\">\n        Expresses ideas or facts in a clear, concise and open manner. Communication indicates a consideration for the feelings and needs of others. Actively listens and proactively shares knowledge. Handles conflict effectively, by overcoming differences of opinion and finding common ground.\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                                    </div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                    </div>\n                </details>\n                    \n                        \n                        \n                        \n            \n            \n                        \n            \n                    \n                        \n                        \n                        \n                                                                                            \n            \n                        \n            \n                            <details class=\"article article--details regular-fields-label--inline article--collapsible article--open\" open>\n                                                                    \n    <summary class=\"article__header \"\n                    aria-controls=\"section9__content\"\n            aria-label=\"Press space or enter keys to toggle section - Education Requirements\"\n            aria-expanded=\"true\"\n            >\n        <div class=\"article__header__text\">\n            <h3 class=\"article__header__text__title title title--h6\"  data-au=\"ag-h3-28\" id=\"section9__title\">\n                Education Requirements\n            </h3>\n\n                    </div>\n\n                    <div class=\"article__header__actions\">\n                <div class=\"toggle toggle--details\">\n                    <span class=\"toggle__icon\" data-tc-icon aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n            </summary>\n\n                    \n                    <div class=\"article__content\" id=\"section9__content\" aria-labelledby=\"section9__title\">\n                        <div class=\"article__content__view\">\n                                                                                                                                    \n                                    <div class=\"article__content__view__field \">\n                                        \n                                        <div class=\"article__content__view__field__value\">\n                                                                                            <style>\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style,\n  #schemaField_font_style * {\n    font-size: inherit !important;\n    font-style: normal !important;\n    color: inherit !important;\n  }\n\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style a {\n    color: #006699 !important;\n  }\n\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style a:hover {\n    color: #006699 !important;\n  }\n</style>\n<div id=\"schemaField_font_style\"><ul><li data-pasted=\"true\">An Advanced University Degree (Master’s Degree or equivalent), &nbsp;with ten (10) years of relevant experience is required; Or</li><li>A First Level University Degree (Bachelor’s Degree or equivalent) with twelve (12) years of relevant experience may be accepted.</li><li>A technical degree or military degree with fourteen (14) years of experience is accepted in lieu of an advanced university degree.</li></ul><p><span style=\"font-family: arial;\">---</span></p><ul><li data-pasted=\"true\" style=\"font-family: arial;\">Un diplôme universitaire supérieur (Master ou équivalent), avec dix (10) ans d'expérience pertinente est requis ; OU</li><li style=\"font-family: arial;\">Un diplôme universitaire de premier cycle (Licence ou équivalent) avec douze (12) ans d'expérience pertinente peut être accepté ; OU</li><li style=\"font-family: arial;\">Un diplôme technique ou un diplôme militaire avec quatorze (14) ans d'expérience est accepté au lieu d'un diplôme universitaire supérieur.</li></ul><p><br></p></div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                                    </div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                    </div>\n                </details>\n                    \n                        \n                        \n <details class=\"article article--details regular-fields-label--inline article--collapsible article--open\" open>\n                                                                    \n    <summary class=\"article__header \"\n                    aria-controls=\"section10__content\"\n            aria-label=\"Press space or enter keys to toggle section - Experience Requirements\"\n            aria-expanded=\"true\"\n            >\n        <div class=\"article__header__text\">\n            <h3 class=\"article__header__text__title title title--h6\"  data-au=\"ag-h3-28\" id=\"section10__title\">\n                Experience Requirements\n            </h3>\n\n                    </div>\n\n                    <div class=\"article__header__actions\">\n                <div class=\"toggle toggle--details\">\n                    <span class=\"toggle__icon\" data-tc-icon aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n            </summary>\n\n                    \n                    <div class=\"article__content\" id=\"section10__content\" aria-labelledby=\"section10__title\">\n                        <div class=\"article__content__view\">\n                                                                                                                                    \n                                    <div class=\"article__content__view__field \">\n                                        \n                                        <div class=\"article__content__view__field__value\">\n                                                                                            <style>\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style,\n  #schemaField_font_style * {\n    font-size: inherit !important;\n    font-style: normal !important;\n    color: inherit !important;\n  }\n\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style a {\n    color: #006699 !important;\n  }\n\n  \n  #schemaField_font_style a:hover {\n    color: #006699 !important;\n  }\n</style>\n<div id=\"schemaField_font_style\">\n<p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\" data-pasted=\"true\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: underline; text-decoration-skip-ink: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Female Candidates are strongly encouraged to apply.&nbsp;</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: underline; text-decoration-skip-ink: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Candidates with no United Nations or UNOPS experience are strongly encouraged to apply.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Required Experience</span></p><ul><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height: 1.2; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Relevant experience is defined as progressively responsible experience in directly leading, designing and/or supporting mediation, negotiations, facilitation, dialogue (design) issues in conflict, post-conflict or prevention settings, and/or experience in mediation advice to chief mediators, senior envoys and/or conflict parties ; academia (research and/or teaching), and/or related fields. A significant part of the ten years of professional experience overall should be in one or more of the thematic areas of: Design and Conduct of Mediation, Facilitation and Dialogue Processes; Security Arrangements (emphasis on ceasefires); Constitutional Issues; Power-sharing; Gender and Inclusion; Climate Change, Environment and Natural Resources; Transitional Justice and Reconciliation ; Digital Technology, Mediation, and Inclusion.</span></li></ul><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Desirable experience (the following experience is optional, candidates who do not have it are welcome to apply.)</span></p><ul><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height: 1.2; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Significant and prolonged field experience in conflict, post-conflict or other transitional situations is desirable.&nbsp;</span></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height: 1.2; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Experience in delivering mediation capacity-building or training activities is an asset.<br>Significant experience in engaging with civil society in peace processes is an asset.</span></li></ul><p><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\" data-pasted=\"true\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Language</span></p><ul><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height: 1.2; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial;\">Working knowledge of oral and written English is required. Application materials must be submitted in English. The cover letter may be submitted in French, accompanied by a translation in English. Working knowledge of at least one other official UN language is highly desirable, in particular Arabic and French.&nbsp;</li></ul><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\">----</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\" data-pasted=\"true\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: underline; text-decoration-skip-ink: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Les candidatures féminines</span><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(34, 34, 34);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: underline; text-decoration-skip-ink: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">&nbsp;sont fortement encouragées.&nbsp;</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(34, 34, 34);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: underline; text-decoration-skip-ink: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Les candidat(e)s qui n'ont aucune expérience de travail à l'ONU ou à l'UNOPS sont fortement encouragé(e)s à appliquer.</span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\" data-pasted=\"true\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Expérience requise&nbsp;</span></p><ul><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height: 1.38; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">L'expérience pertinente est définie comme une expérience à responsabilités croissantes dans la conduite directe, la conception et/ou l'appui à des processus de médiation, de négociation, de facilitation et de dialogue (conception) dans des contextes de conflit, d'après-conflit ou de prévention ; et/ou une expérience en conseil en médiation auprès de médiateurs principaux, d'envoyés de haut niveau et/ou de parties au conflit ; ou encore dans le milieu universitaire (recherche et/ou enseignement) et/ou dans des domaines connexes. Une part substantielle de ces dix années d'expérience professionnelle globale doit relever d'un ou de plusieurs des domaines thématiques: Conduite et conception de processus de médiation, de facilitation et de dialogue; Dispositifs de sécurité (notamment les cessez-le-feu); Questions constitutionnelles; &nbsp;Partage du pouvoir; Genre et inclusivité; Changement climatique et ressources naturelles; Justice transitionnelle et réconciliation; Technologie numérique, médiation et inclusion.</span></li></ul><p><span style=\"font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;margin-right: 30pt;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(34, 34, 34);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Expérience souhaitable (Ces expériences sont souhaitables. Les candidat(e)s qui n'ont aucune de ces expériences sont aussi encouragé(e)s à appliquer)</span></p><ul><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height: 1.2; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Une importante expérience de terrain dans des contextes de conflit, de sortie de conflit ou de transition.&nbsp;</span></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height: 1.2; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Une partie importante des 10 années d’expérience professionnelle doit être dans une ou plusieurs des domaines thématiques.</span></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height: 1.2; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Expérience directe dans la fourniture de conseils en matière de médiation aux médiateurs principaux, envoyés seniors, et / ou les parties au conflit est un atout.</span></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height: 1.2; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Expérience dans l'exécution des activités de renforcement des capacités de médiation ou de formation est un atout.</span></li><li dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height: 1.2; text-align: justify; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; font-family: arial; font-size: 12pt;\"><span style=\"color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: 400; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Expérience significative d'engagement avec la société civile dans les processus de paix est un atout.</span></li></ul><p><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial;\"><br></span></p><p dir=\"ltr\" style=\"line-height:1.2;text-align: justify;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt;\" data-pasted=\"true\"><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial; color: rgb(0, 0, 0);  font-weight: 700; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-decoration: none; vertical-align: baseline; white-space: pre-wrap;\">Compétences linguistiques</span></p><ul><li><span style=\"font-size: 12pt; font-family: arial;\">Une connaissance pratique de l’anglais écrit et parlé est requise. Le dossier de candidature doit être présenté en anglais. La lettre de motivation peut être présentée en français avec une traduction en anglais. Une connaissance pratique d'au moins une autre langue officielle de l'ONU est hautement désirable, en particulier l'arabe et le français.</span></li></ul>\n</div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                                    </div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                    </div>\n                </details>\n                    \n                        \n                        \n                        \n                                                                                            \n            \n                        \n            \n                            <details class=\"article article--details regular-fields-label--inline article--collapsible article--open\" open>\n                                                                    \n    <summary class=\"article__header \"\n                    aria-controls=\"section11__content\"\n            aria-label=\"Press space or enter keys to toggle section - Language Requirements\"\n            aria-expanded=\"true\"\n            >\n        <div class=\"article__header__text\">\n            <h3 class=\"article__header__text__title title title--h6\"  data-au=\"ag-h3-28\" id=\"section11__title\">\n                Language Requirements\n            </h3>\n\n                    </div>\n\n                    <div class=\"article__header__actions\">\n                <div class=\"toggle toggle--details\">\n                    <span class=\"toggle__icon\" data-tc-icon aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n            </summary>\n\n                    \n                    <div class=\"article__content\" id=\"section11__content\" aria-labelledby=\"section11__title\">\n                        <div class=\"article__content__view\">\n                                                                                                                                    \n                                    <div class=\"article__content__view__field \">\n                                        \n                                        <div class=\"article__content__view__field__value\">\n                                                                                            <table class=\"schemaspec item_816\"><colgroup><col class=\"item_816_col_1\"><col class=\"item_816_col_2\"><col class=\"item_816_col_3\"></colgroup><thead><th>Language</th><th>Proficiency Level</th><th>Requirement</th></thead><tbody><tr><td>English</td><td>Fluent</td><td>Required</td></tr><tr><td>French</td><td>Fluent</td><td>Desirable</td></tr><tr><td>Spanish</td><td>Fluent</td><td>Desirable</td></tr><tr><td>Chinese, Mandarin</td><td>Fluent</td><td>Desirable</td></tr><tr><td>Russian</td><td>Fluent</td><td>Desirable</td></tr><tr><td>Arabic</td><td>Fluent</td><td>Desirable</td></tr></tbody></table>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                                    </div>\n                                                                                    </div>\n                    </div>\n                </details>\n                    \n                        \n                        \n                        \n                                                                                            \n            \n                        \n            \n                            <details class=\"article article--details regular-fields-label--inline article--collapsible article--open\" open>\n                                                                    \n    <summary class=\"article__header \"\n                    aria-controls=\"section12__content\"\n            aria-label=\"Press space or enter keys to toggle section - Additional Information\"\n            aria-expanded=\"true\"\n            >\n        <div class=\"article__header__text\">\n            <h3 class=\"article__header__text__title title title--h6\"  data-au=\"ag-h3-28\" id=\"section12__title\">\n                Additional Information\n            </h3>\n\n                    </div>\n\n                    <div class=\"article__header__actions\">\n                <div class=\"toggle toggle--details\">\n                    <span class=\"toggle__icon\" data-tc-icon aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n            </summary>\n\n                    \n                    <div class=\"article__content\" id=\"section12__content\" aria-labelledby=\"section12__title\">\n                        <div class=\"article__content__view\">\n                                                                                                                                    \n                                    <div class=\"article__content__view__field \">\n                                        \n                                        <div class=\"article__content__view__field__value\">\n                                                                                            <style>\n  .justified-content {\n    text-align: justify !important;\n  }\n\n  .justified-content ul {\n    list-style: disc inside;\n    margin: 0;\n    padding-left: 1em;\n  }\n\n  .justified-content li {\n    margin-bottom: 0.5em;\n  }\n\n  .justified-content div {\n    margin: 0.75em 0 0.25em 0;\n  }\n</style>\n\n<div><ul><li>UNOPS does not accept unsolicited resumes.&nbsp;</li><li>UNOPS will at no stage of the recruitment process request candidates to make payments of any kind.</li><li>Applications to vacancies must be received before midnight Copenhagen time (CET) on the closing date of the announcement. Applications received after the closing date will not be considered.&nbsp;</li><li>Only shortlisted candidates will be contacted and invited to proceed to the next stage of the selection process, which may include various assessments.&nbsp;</li><li>UNOPS embraces diversity and is committed to equal employment opportunity. Our workforce consists of a wide range of nationalities, cultures, languages, races, gender identities, sexual orientations, and abilities. We strive to sustain and strengthen this diversity, fostering an inclusive working environment where all personnel are treated with respect and have equal access to opportunities.</li><li>UNOPS evaluates all applications based on the skills, qualifications and experience outlined in the vacancy announcement. We are committed to a fair and transparent selection process and welcome diverse perspectives, including those of women, indigenous and racialized communities, individuals of diverse gender identities and sexual orientations, and persons with disabilities.</li><li>We are committed to enabling all candidates to perform at their best during the assessment process. If you are shortlisted and require support or reasonable accommodation to complete any assessment, please inform our human resources team upon receiving your invitation.</li><li>UNOPS has zero tolerance for sexual exploitation and abuse (SEA), sexual harassment, and other forms of abusive conduct, including discrimination, abuse of authority, and harassment. To uphold these standards, background checks are conducted for all final candidates to help ensure that individuals with a history of such conduct are not hired. By applying for a position with UNOPS, candidates acknowledge and consent to these verification processes.<br></li></ul>&nbsp;<br><strong><font size=\"4\">Terms and Conditions</font></strong></div><div>&nbsp;</div><div><ul><li>For staff positions only, UNOPS reserves the right to appoint a candidate at a lower level than the advertised level of the post.&nbsp;</li><li>For retainer contracts, you must complete a few mandatory courses (they take around 4 hours to complete) in your own time, before providing services to UNOPS. Refreshers or new mandatory courses may be required during your contract. Please note that you will not receive any compensation for taking courses and refreshers. For more information on a retainer contract <a href=\"https://drive.google.com/file/d/13FXRi20MZ0a49R-l82qT0OA0cX_5Bj3e/view?usp=sharing\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener\">here</a>.&nbsp;</li><li>\n  For more details about the contract types, please&nbsp;<a href=\"https://drive.google.com/file/d/15TJf0e2aJzEGewuEPWdGbPWsFoPjfUr8/view?usp=drive_link\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer noopener\"><font color=\"#33ccff\">click here.</font></a></li><li>All UNOPS personnel are responsible for performing their duties in accordance with the UN Charter and UNOPS Policies and Instructions, as well as other relevant accountability frameworks. In addition, all personnel must demonstrate an understanding of the Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs) in a manner consistent with UN core values and the UN Common Agenda.&nbsp;</li><li>It is the policy of UNOPS to conduct background checks on all potential personnel. Recruitment in UNOPS is contingent on the results of such checks.</li></ul></div><style>\n  .justified-content {\n    text-align: justify !important;\n  }\nPlease note that UNOPS does not accept unsolicited resumes. \nApplications received after the closing date will not be considered. \nPlease note that only shortlisted candidates will be contacted and advance to the next stage of the selection process, which involves various assessments. \nUNOPS embraces diversity and is committed to equal employment opportunity. Our workforce consists of many diverse nationalities, cultures, languages, races, gender identities, sexual orientations, and abilities. UNOPS seeks to sustain and strengthen this diversity to ensure equal opportunities as well as an inclusive working environment for its entire workforce. \nQualified women and candidates from groups which are underrepresented in the UNOPS workforce are encouraged to apply. These include in particular candidates from racialized and/or indigenous groups, members of minority gender identities and sexual orientations, and people with disabilities. \nWe would like to ensure all candidates perform at their best during the assessment process. If you are shortlisted and require additional assistance to complete any assessment, including reasonable accommodation, please inform our human resources team when you receive an invitation. \nTerms and Conditions \nFor staff positions only, UNOPS reserves the right to appoint a candidate at a lower level than the advertised level of the post. \nFor retainer contracts, you must complete a few mandatory courses (they take around 4 hours to complete) in your own time, before providing services to UNOPS. Refreshers or new mandatory courses may be required during your contract. Please note that you will not receive any compensation for taking courses and refreshers. For more information on a retainer contract here. \nAll UNOPS personnel are responsible for performing their duties in accordance with the UN Charter and UNOPS Policies and Instructions, as well as other relevant accountability frameworks.
